# Grunt
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows
[Grunt]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 54
Spawn Location: [[Mithril Deposits]]
Special Behavior: Summons Silvo
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 15,000; [[Damage]]: 300
[Drops]
Drops: 8–15x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 8–15x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Treasurite]]
XP: +81 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 59
[Metadata]
ID: TEAM_TREASURITE_GRUNT_50
Grunt is a [[Mob]] found in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] and a member of [[Team Treasurite]]. It throws a ball that spawns a [[Silvo]].
## Spawning
Grunts have a 76.34% (1 in 1.31) chance to spawn instead of other [[Team Treasurite]] members in the [[Mithril Deposits]].
## Behavior
When encountered, a Grunt will throw a ball that spawns a [[Silvo]], which is a silverfish with 6,500 [[Health]] that deals 300 [[Damage]]. Both the Grunt and Silvo will attempt to approach the player and attack. After a few seconds, the Silvo will despawn. The Grunt can spawn another Silvo by throwing a ball again.

## Trivia
The ball thrown by Team Treasurite members to summon their associated creature
- The mobs are based on Team Rocket from Pokémon.
  - The ball that each mob throws in battle resembles Dive Ball in Pokémon.
  - The Grunt looks like a Team Rocket Grunt, Executive Sebastian looks like Proton, Executive Wendy looks like Ariana, and Executive Viper is most likely Archer. Corleone, as the leader, is probably the counterpart of Giovanni. Within Pokémon, Executives are the highest ranking members of Team Rocket after Giovanni.
  - The Grunt's [[Bestiary]] entry, "Team Treasurite is up to no good. Prepare for trouble!" references Jessie and James' catchphrase originating from the Pokémon anime series.

# Headless Horseman
Aliases: Horseman
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Events, Spooky Festival, Bosses
[Headless Horseman]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 259
Spawn Location: [[Graveyard]] [[Wilderness]]
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Skeletal]] [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 3,000,000; [[Defense]]: 25; [[Damage]]: 600
[Drops]
Drops: 0–2x [[Green Candy]] 0–2x [[Purple Candy]] 4x [[Spooky Fragment]](The player who spawned it) 1x [[Spooky Fragment]](1st Place) 0–1x [[Spooky Fragment]](2nd Place) 0–1x [[Midnight Dye]]
XP: +1,942 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 777
[Metadata]
ID: HEADLESS_HORSEMAN_100; HORSEMAN_HORSE_100
The Headless Horseman is a [[Mob]] and [[Bosses|BOSS]] spawned by using the [[Horseman's Candle]].
## Spawning
The Headless Horseman is spawned when a [[Horseman's Candle]] is used under valid conditions. Horseman's Candles can be purchased from the [[Fear Mongerer]] for 32 [[Purple Candy]].
The Candle must be used under valid conditions in order to spawn the boss:
- It must be nighttime (7pm - 5am).
- It must be used in the [[Graveyard]] or [[Wilderness]] on the [[Hub Island]].
### Stranded Mode
The Headless Horseman can be spawned on the [[Private Island]] instead.
## Behavior
The Headless Horseman constantly attacks the nearest player rapidly, dealing lots of damage. It also possesses two abilities:
- Headless Zombie Swarm: Summons a horde of 4 baby [[Headless Zombie|Headless Zombies]] with 20,000 [[Health]] each. They will attack the nearest player.
- Bat Swarm: Summons a horde of [[Bat (Headless Horseman)|Bats]] which throw [[Damage Potion|Damage II]] and [[Wounded Potion|Wounded II]] potions at nearby players. The [[Damage Potion|Damage II]] potions deal 10 [[Damage]] and [[Wounded Potion|Wounded II]] potions reduce [[Healing]] by 50% for 1 minute.
The Headless Horseman cannot be damaged by [[Arrows]] and the [[Bonemerang]]. Attempting to damage it with a projectile will reel the player towards the boss and blind them for 3 seconds.
## Drops
Non-item drops - [Lv259]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Coins]]|647|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|1,942|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|777|
Upon death, the Headless Horseman turns the ground under its death point to obsidian in a 2 block radius.
A message is displayed in chat announcing to the server that it has been defeated, who dealt the final blow, the top 3 damagers, and the player's damage, position, and gained [[Runecrafting]] XP and Bits, similar to the message displayed when a boss dies. There is also a beacon that displays the location of the boss's defeat: its drops appear around the beacon in the obsidian radius.
Loot Table:
|Slot|Item|Quality Requirement|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Green Candy]]|0–2 [[Green Candy]]|5||
|[[Purple Candy]]|0–2 [[Purple Candy]]|18||
|[[Spooky Fragment]]|[[Spooky Fragment]]|60|The player who summons the Headless Horseman will receive 4 Spooky Fragments ([[Spooky Fragment]]).; The player who places first in damage will receive 1 [[Spooky Fragment]].; The player who places second in damage will receive 0–1 [[Spooky Fragment]].|
||5 [[Bits]]||Can only be received with a Cookie Buff ([[Booster Cookie]]) active.|
Additionally, up to 150 [[Runecrafting]] XP can be obtained.

## Bugs
- When spawned during the [[Spooky Festival]], the Headless Horseman does not respect the player's invincibility frames, meaning it can kill them very quickly.
  - This bug does not happen during the hour before and hour after the Spooky Festival while the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the [[Village]].
## Trivia
Runic Headless Horseman
- The Headless Horseman, unlike most other bosses, can spawn as [[Runecrafting|Runic]]. Its runic version has only 10,000,000 [[Health]] instead of the expected 12,000,000 [[Health]] .
- The Headless Horseman technically has a head; however, its head is a jack-o'-lantern, similar to its original appearance in The Legend of Sleepy Hollow, where the Headless Horseman originated.
- It was possible to use the Headless Horseman to remove [[Bedrock]] on the player's [[Private Island]] on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], but this was later patched.
- The Headless Horseman gives an average profit of for the summoner, or for a group of three.
  - It therefore costs to complete the [[Bestiary]] alone, or as a group.

ID: HELLWISP_100
The Hellwisp is a pack of upside-down [[Ghast|Ghasts]] which attempts to pull the player in with its tentacles.
## Spawning
Two Hellwisps appear in two of four lava pools in one of the northwest areas leading off from the [[Magma Chamber]], outside of the [[Matriarch's Lair]].
## Behavior
When a nearby player approaches it, the Hellwisp will shoot out a tentacle which traps the player. It will then bring the player into its mouth, and will quickly hurt them.

A Hydrospear is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Drowned Reliquary]].
## Spawning
Hydrospears can be found in the [[Drowned Reliquary]], accessed through the [[Reefguard Pass]] from the [[Murkwater Depths]].
## Behavior
Hydrospear slowly swims towards the player to hit them. Every few seconds Hydrospear throws an axe towards the player that deals 500 [[Damage]].

# Ice Dragon
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Ice Dragon]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 765
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]; [[Ender]]; [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000; [[Defense]]: 2,500; [[Damage]]: 720,000
[Drops]
XP: +1,147 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 841
[Metadata]
ID: MASTER_WITHER_KING_DRAGON_100
The Ice Dragon, also named Withered Dragon, is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the final phase of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss fight.
## Boss Fight
The bottom of Master Mode Floor VII contains a giant throne room. There are five Relics placed next to Dragon statues around the room. There are also five cauldrons in front of the throne near the center of the room. They each have a specifically colored Clay below them. The players must bring and place the Relics with the same corresponding color as the Cauldron's block of clay. By doing so, The Wither King will be summoned on his throne.
Damage cannot be dealt towards The Wither King, and he mostly acts as a spectator to the battle. The Wither King spawns five dragons with special abilities, including the Ice Dragon. All five dragons must be killed near their corresponding colored Dragon statue to destroy them. All Dragons can respawn at any time indefinitely until all dragon statues are destroyed. If all five statues are destroyed, the floor is completed.
## Behavior
The Ice Dragon will fly close to the floor, turning it into Ice and dealing [[Damage]] to nearby players or trapping them inside cubes of Ice. Standing on the Ice will slow down players.

## Behavior
### Boss Stats

### Demon Stats

|IV|10M [[Health]]|25,000|Trail of Fire The demon leaves a trail of fire behind him dealing 1,350 [[True Damage]] if you touch it.|
### Tier I
- Immolate - The Inferno Demonlord shoots fireballs to its target rapidly, only dealing [[Knockback]]. When the boss is alive, it deals [[True Damage]] depending on its health if players fighting it goes into its aggro range.
- Demonsplit - The boss splits into demons at 50% HP (1,250,000), named Quazii and Typhoeus (a Wither Skeleton and a Pigman appearance respectively). Upon spawning, only Quazii can be damaged. Typhoeus will be immune. Quazii will behave more aggressively, whereas Typhoeus, while immune, will be repelled by the player when within 5 blocks of them. When Quazii is defeated, Typhoeus will adopt more aggressive behavior and can be attacked. When Typhoeus is defeated, the Demonlord reforms and resumes attacking.
### Tier II
- Hellion Shield - The boss has 99% Damage reduction from all sources. Only direct strikes from Blaze Slayer Daggers ([[Twilight Dagger]], [[Firedust Dagger]], [[Kindlebane Dagger]], [[Mawdredge Dagger]], [[Pyrochaos Dagger]], [[Deathripper Dagger]]) attuned identically to the attunement of the boss's shield, enchantment damage from those strikes will ignore the shield and deal full damage to the boss. This excludes [[Ferocity]], so [[Ferocity]] upgrades are ineffective. Every 8 strikes with the correct attunement, the shield will change its attunement, in order of Ashen > Spirit > Auric > Crystal then it goes back to Ashen. All other attacks have their damage reduced by aforementioned 99%.
  - Enchantment damage is not mitigated, with the exception of [[Venomous]]. [[Thunderlord]], [[Crimson Armor|Crimson Swipes]], and [[Fire Aspect]] deal their full damage through Hellion Shield.
- Demonsplit - The Demonlord splits into its demons once at 50% HP (5,000,000). The demons behave as before, but now also have shields, which similarly to Demonlord change every 8 hits. Quazii will alternate only between Spirit and Crystal, while Typhoeus alternates only between Ashen and Auric. When a shield switches, the immunity between Quazii and Typhoeus also switches. Regardless the immunity, both demons walks towards their target, but whoever gets the immunity keeps walking and retreating within a 5 block radius of the target. For example, when Quazii is spawned with Spirit and is hit correspondingly 8 times, Quazii becomes immune to damage, leaves and Typhoeus grants Ashen. Once Typhoeus is hit correspondingly 8 times, Typhoeus becomes immune to damage, leaves and Quazii grants Crystal. This process goes on until the demons are both defeated and the Inferno Demonlord reforms and resumes attacking.
- Fire Pillars - Beginning at 50% HP, every 20 seconds, the boss will prepare a fireball above its head for 3.5 seconds. Then, it will throw it in a random direction, where a 4-block high orange pillar will form. Such pillars must be destroyed within 7 seconds of being placed. The pillar has no invincibility frames. The pillar gets 1 block shorter every two times it is hit until at 8 hits, it is no longer present. If it is not destroyed, all players fighting the Demonlord will take 1000 × Max [[Health]] as [[True Damage]]. This can be blocked by using the [[Wither Cloak Sword]] and [[Mithril Coat]]'s abilities.
### Tier III
- Demonsplit - The Demonlord splits into its demons twice, at 2/3 HP and 1/3 HP.
  - ROFLcopter - Quazii gains a beam which spins slowly and is centered on Quazii's hitbox. If the player touches the beam, they will deal 1,500 [[True Damage]], and Quazii will heal 20% of its health (1,000,000 hp at Tier III, and 2,000,000 hp at Tier IV).
- Fire Pillars - Fire pillars begin at 2/3 HP (30,000,000).
- DDR Apocalypse - Beginning at 1/3 HP (15,000,000), the boss will spawn up to 5 fire pits near the player regularly. Orange terracotta blocks will form in small cross shapes near the player, which turn to fire after 1.5 seconds. The fire deals 950 [[True Damage]] per second. Note that fire pits spawn immediately before the demons spawn, as well as immediately after.
### Tier IV
The Demonlord functions identically to previous tiers.
- Demonsplit - It splits into its demons twice, at 2/3 HP (100,000,000) and 1/3 HP (50,000,000).
  - Trail of Fire - Typhoeus gains a trail of fire following it. The fire deals 1,350 [[True Damage]] if contact is made.
- Fire Pillars - Fire pillars begin spawning at 2/3 HP (100,000,000).
- DDR Apocalypse - At 1/3 HP (50,000,000), the boss spawns up to 5 fire pits near the player at regular intervals though it deals 2,865 [[True Damage]] per second.
## Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 20% (1 in 5) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.
|Mob|Stats|Spawned at/via|Mob Types|Item Drops|Non-Item Drops|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv381] [[Flare Demon]]|[[Health]]: 8,750,000; [[Damage]]: 3,000|Tier III [[Blaze Slayer]]|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|1x [[Derelict Ashe]]|+2,857 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 1143 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv456] [[Kindleheart Demon]]|[[Health]]: 17,500,000; [[Damage]]: 6,000|Tier IV [[Blaze Slayer]]|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|2x [[Derelict Ashe]]|+3,420 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 1368 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv522] [[Burningsoul Demon]]|[[Health]]: 35,000,000; [[Damage]]: 9,000|Tier IV [[Blaze Slayer]]|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|5x [[Derelict Ashe]]|+3,915 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 1566 [[Experience]]|
## Tips
- While the boss does [[True Damage]] its demons deal high amount of regular [[Damage]], therefore prioritizing one over the other too much will not work against the Slayer.
- If the player can deal high and rapid damage, the slayer boss will not be able to throw any fire pillars before the split or death. Damage nullification items like [[Spirit Mask]] or Phoenix Pet should be prepared in case the player fails to deal enough damage in time or fails to breaking pillar.
  - Due to the Hellion Shield mitigation of [[Venomous]] damage, the [[One For All]] ultimate enchant will prove to be the highest DPS option for many players, heavily assisting in the "DPS-Race" style of this slayer.
- Using [[Gemstone|Opal Gemstone]] on combat slots of armor and daggers can help massively for nullifying the [[True Damage]] boss deals, if more survivability is needed.
- When boss uses DDR-Apocalypse ability, blocks that will be lit will be marked on the ground, so make sure to avoid marked blocks.
## Trivia
- Because of vanilla Minecraft AI and abilities, Typhoeus is stronger than Quazii. Therefore, the [[Firedust Dagger]], [[Kindlebane Dagger]] and [[Pyrochaos Dagger]] are respectively stronger than the [[Twilight Dagger]], [[Mawdredge Dagger]] and [[Deathripper Dagger]] if the Burning Vengeance ﬗ ability is counted.
- If both Quazii and Typhoeus spawn in a place which is one block large and get pushed, Quazii always gets pushed into the wall and take suffocation damage first.
- Contrary to its description, the DDR Apocalypse fire's damage is based on the player's current [[Health]], not their max [[Health]].
- Despite being classified as [[True Damage]], the damage of the demons' ROFLcopter and Trail of Fire is unaffected by [[True Defense]].

Kalhuiki Elders are mobs that spawn in the [[Jungle]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. They deal 800,000 [[Damage]], and attack players if the player attacks a nearby [[Kalhuiki Youngling]]. They have 1,000,000,000 [[Health]] and drop nothing when killed.
Unlike [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member|Kalhuiki Tribe Members]] which can spawn anywhere in the jungle biome, Elders only spawn in the jungle village where [[Odawa]] is also located.
## Spawning
Kalhuiki Elders spawn in the village structure where [[Odawa]] is located, found within the [[Jungle]].
## Behavior
Kalhuiki Elders are passive until the player hits a [[Kalhuiki Youngling]], after which they will attempt to attack the player, dealing 800,000 [[Damage]] on contact.

Kalhuiki Tribe Members are mobs that spawn in the [[Jungle Temple]] and [[Jungle]], and will deal 800,000 [[Damage]]. They have 1B [[Health]] , and drop nothing when killed.
## Spawning
Kalhuiki Tribe Members spawn in the [[Jungle]] and [[Jungle Temple]].
## Behavior
Kalhuiki Tribe Members are passive until the player hits one of them 3 times, or hits a [[Kalhuiki Youngling]], after which they will attempt to attack the player, dealing 800,000 [[Damage]] on contact.
They will not attack the player if the player is wearing a [[Kalhuiki Mask]].

## Spawning

## Behavior

If they are attacked, nearby [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member|Kalhuiki Tribe Members]] and [[Kalhuiki Elder|Kalhuiki Elders]] will attack the player, unless the player is wearing a [[Kalhuiki Mask]].

The Key Guardian is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] found in the [[Jungle]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]]. It has 250,000 [[Health]] and drops a [[Jungle Key]] when killed.
## Location
The Key Guardian can be found in two [[Granite]] towers in the [[Jungle]]. It has a 1m 30s cooldown between spawning if there is a moving player in the structure where it spawns, and does not spawn if there are no players in the structure or if all players are not moving. Looting affects the chance to receive 2 or more Jungle Keys.

[[Experience]] Orbs: 371
[Metadata]
ID: KING_MINOS_1750
King Minos is a [[Mythological]] creature that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A King Minos has a chance to spawn when digging [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
|Griffin Pet Rarity|Prefix|Level|Stats|Spawn Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Empyrean|[Lv1,750]|100,000,000 [[Health]]; 2,500 [[Damage]]|0.26%; (1 in 385.33 mobs)|
[[Tracking]] increases the chance to spawn King Minos.
## Behavior
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
While fighting King Minos, your [[Vitality]] is halved.
### Shield Phase
King Minos spawns riding an orange sheep with a 75 hit shield, similar to the [[Voidgloom Seraph]], during which the boss does not take damage and every hit reduces the shield's hits by one.
During this phase, King Minos can attack by ramming into a player, dealing damage and knocking the player straight into the air, or casting his fishing rod at a player and trapping them in place if the bobber hits them.
Once the shield is broken, King Minos will dismount the sheep and enter the next phase, where he can start taking damage.
### Damage Phase
In this phase, a rage meter appears above King Minos. This rage meter increments by 1% whenever King Minos takes damage or deals damage to the player. At 30% onwards, it will turn red, signaling that King Minos is enraged enough to use a high-damage area of effect attack centered on himself. The amount of rage he has (in %) converts his damage stat into [[True Damage]]. For example, at 50% rage, King Minos deals 1,250 damage and 1,250 true damage. King Minos' Rage caps at 100%.
King Minos can summon lightning on the block which the player is standing on, dealing high damage.
If the player is out of range, King Minos will switch to attacking with a bow.

King Minos can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[King Minos Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

## Tips
- A [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] can be used to skip the last 10% of the fight if survival becomes difficult.
  - However, it should be noted that in this case, swapping to a pet other than a [[Griffin Pet|Griffin]] before it dies will result in receiving no loot other than the shards.
- In group fights, the spawner should fall back once the rage meter turns red to avoid despawning the boss on death.
- It is recommended to bring an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]] to escape the hook attack.

[Metadata]
ID: GOBLIN_KNIFE_THROWER_25
The Knifethrower is a type of [[Mob]] and [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblin]] that spawns in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Knifethrowers spawn naturally in the [[Goblin Burrows]] of the [[Dwarven Mines]] and the [[Goblin Holdout]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Behavior
Knifethrowers throw knives as ranged attacks.
## Drops
Knifethrowers have a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] (5%) chance to drop a bag of coins holding 500–10,000 [[Coins]]. The player will be notified in the chat over the drop and the amount.

# Krondor Necromancer
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Crimson Isle
[Krondor Necromancer]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 245
Spawn Location: [[Mage Outpost]]
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]]; [[Arcane]]; Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 5,000,000; [[Damage]]: 4,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]]
XP: +367 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 269
[Metadata]
ID: KRONDOR_NECROMANCER_80
The Krondor Necromancer is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Mage Outpost]].
## Spawning
The Krondor Necromancer can rarely spawn in the [[Mage Outpost]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv245]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Cyclamen Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.00004%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv245]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|81|81|81|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|367|367|367|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|269|269|269|
Killing a Krondor Necromancer when the player is in the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] will reward them with [[Factions|25 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]]. If the player is in the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] and they kill a Krondor Necromancer, they will lose [[Factions|5 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
## Behavior
When hostile, the Krondor Necromancer will begin to shoot fireballs towards the player every 3 seconds, which deal 4,000 [[Damage]] on hit. If the player is close to the Krondor Necromancer, it will begin to run towards the player, and shoot fireballs at a much higher rate.
## Trivia
Krondor Necromancer shares the same skin with the [[Mage Outlaw]].
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Kuudra Berserker is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Kuudra Berserkers occasionally spawn in batches during new waves in the [[Kuudra]] fight. They can spawn starting from the second wave.
## Behavior
Kuudra Berserkers will quickly run towards nearby players to attack them. They will spawn an [[Explosive Imp]] when killed.

## Spawning
Kuudra Followers frequently spawn in batches during new waves in the [[Kuudra]] fight. They can spawn starting from the first wave.
## Behavior
Kuudra Followers will quickly run towards nearby players to attack them. They deal 50% less [[Damage]] with a [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]], or 65% less with a [[Kuudra Follower Relic]].

The Kuudra Knocker is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Kuudra Knockers occasionally spawn in batches during new waves in the [[Kuudra]] fight. They can spawn starting from the fourth wave.
## Behavior
Kuudra Knockers will run towards nearby players to attack them, dealing immense [[Knockback]]. This can also knock players off of cannons.

# Lagoon Leech
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
[Lagoon Leech]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 2
Spawn Location: [[Black Lagoon]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 80; [[Rift Damage]]: 12.5
[Drops]
Drops: 6–12x [[Lil' Pad]]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: LAGOON_LEECH_2
The Lagoon Leech is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Black Lagoon]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv2]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lil' Pad]]|6|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Lil' Pad]]|3|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Lil' Pad]]|3|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv2]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|30|30|30|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
## Behavior
Lagoon Leeches spawn once the player enters water in the [[Black Lagoon]]. They will pursue the player, and move extremely quickly in water. If the player is on land, the Lagoon Leech will follow them in water and then leap up onto the land, where they will move slower. They remove 12.5 [[Rift Time]] on contact.
If the Lagoon Leech re-enters water after walking on land while the player is on land, it will despawn.
### Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Spawning|A Lagoon Leech is swimming towards you! GET OUT OF THE WATER!|
|Attacking|Lagoon Leech removed 12.5sф from you using Devour!|
|Attacking in water|GET OUT OF THE WATER! You're being eaten alive!|
|Despawning|POOF! The leech swam back to a better world!|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Lagoon Leech.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Lagoon Leech.|

The Lapis Zombie is a hostile [[Mob]] that appears as a [[Zombie]], wearing Blue Dyed Leather Armor and using [[Stained Glass|Blue Stained Glass]] as a Helmet. It spawns in the [[Lapis Quarry]] section of the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Spawning
|Location|Spawn Spots (The number of spots in the location where this mob can spawn in.)|Spawn Delay (The time, after which a new mob spawns in every spot.)|Max per spot (The maximum number of mobs that can exist in each spot at a time.)|

|[[Lapis Quarry]]|19|8s|1|
## Behavior
The Lapis Zombie mainly attacks the player like a normal [[Zombie]], with a melee attack. Occasionally it shoots the player with a laser beam, similar to a Guardian.
## Drops

Lapis Zombie can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Lapis Zombie Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+750 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Tips
- Lapis Zombies drop the [[Lapis Armor]], a good starter armor to grind [[Experience]] from Mining. As they spawn in large quantities, it makes it easy to get the full set.
- Lapis Zombies have a [[Drop Chance|10RNGesus Loot]] chance to drop an [[Exp Share Core]]. If the player gets this early on, they can get a bit of money.

# Larva
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Mob pages missing internal IDs
[Larva]
Type: [[Mob]]
Spawn Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Mob Type: None
The Larva is a [[Mob]] in the [[Wyld Woods]].
## Drops
The player can use a [[Larva Hook]] on a Larva to reduce its body by one segment. Once all 3 segments have been removed, the player is rewarded with a [[Larva Silk]].
If the player has a [[Harmonious Surgery Toolkit]], and they touch a larva another player has touched, once it is harvested, they will get +1 [[Larva Silk]].
## Behavior
Larvae can be found wriggling along and around the tree branches in the [[Wyld Woods]] aimlessly. They can move both horizontally and vertically.

# Leech Supreme
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
[Leech Supreme]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 10
Spawn Location: [[Leeches Lair]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 800
[Drops]
Drops: 8x [[Leech Supreme Fragment]]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: LEECH_SUPREME_10
Leech Supreme is a [[Bosses|Boss]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Spawning
Leech Supreme spawns in the [[Leeches Lair]], which the player can access by talking to [[Dr. Edwin]] after completing his quest. [[Dr. Edwin]] will only let the player into the [[Leeches Lair]] if Leech Supreme is alive. Leech Supreme will respawn 40 seconds after it has been killed.
## Drops
When Leech Supreme dies, players fighting it will receive:
Item drops - [Lv10]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Leech Supreme Fragment]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv10]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|3,000|3,000|3,000|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
## Behavior
- Leech Supreme will normally try to hit the player with melee attacks.
- Every time it takes 160 [[Damage]] (at 640 [[Health]] , 480 [[Health]] , 320 [[Health]] , and 160 [[Health]] ), it will fly to the side of the arena, become invulnerable, and enter a different phase.
- Every attack takes a percentage of the player's remaining [[Rift Time]].
These phases are in order:
1. Slime Pound (640 [[Health]] ): a Slime Ball floats to a random location in the arena and slams down, creating a shockwave that takes away the player's [[Rift Time]]. The player can jump over the shockwave. Leech Supreme uses the attack 7 times.
2. Wicked Bombs (480 [[Health]] ): Throws bombs into the arena, with explosion radii being marked by green clay on the arena floor. Bombs alternate between being thrown both randomly in the arena and thrown directly at the player. Bombs take away [[Rift Time]] from players in their explosion radius.
3. Leech Swarm (320 [[Health]] ): Spawns [[Leech Swarm|Leech Swarms]] and [[Leech Alpha|Leech Alphas]] in the arena. More mobs are spawned for each player that is fighting Leech Supreme.
4. Mortiferous Lazer (160 [[Health]] ): Creates a laser and small hole in the arena which is marked with green particles. The laser starts on the side which Leech Supreme is standing on and moves across the arena at a height the player can jump over, then rises one block and moves back across, taking [[Rift Time]] away from players it makes contact with. The player must first jump over the laser, then avoid it by crouching in the hole in the arena. Each time the laser moves down and back, the hole changes position. Leech Supreme uses the attack 3.5 times (4 jumps, 3 ducks).
After Leech Supreme is defeated, the player will be transported to the [[Lagoon Hut]].
## Tips
- Slime Pound and Mortiferous Lazer phases can be negated by standing at [[Coordinates|-146.5, 31, 71.5]].
- If intending on beating Leech Supreme in a group, only one player should talk to [[Dr. Edwin]] and enter the arena, until the end of the Leech Swarm phase as the amount of leeches scales dynamically with the amount of players that entered [[Leeches Lair]].
## Trivia
- Nearly all phases could be cheesed by standing on the ledge of the arena walls.
- Players used to be able to escape the arena.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Leech Supreme.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Leech Supreme.|

# Littlefoot

ID: LITTLEFOOT_533
Littlefoot is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that can be found in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. They have high [[Defense]], but take more damage based on the [[Breaking Power]] of the tool used to attack them.
## Spawning
Littlefoot has a 1% (1 in 100) chance to replace any mob in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. It has a 100% chance to spawn in the Littlefoot's Den variant of the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
## Behavior
When the player approaches Littlefoot, it attempts to run away at high speeds. After being hit once, Littlefoot enrages for about 3 seconds before running at the player and using melee attacks, each dealing -3 [[Cold]]. Enraging sends the following message in chat to the player that enraged it: Looks like you've angered Littlefoot!
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv508]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|

|[[Littlefoot Fluff]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.2%|

|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1,270|1,270|1,270|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|3,810|3,810|3,810|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1,524|1,524|1,524|
|[[Glacite Powder]]|10,000|10,000|10,000|
Littlefoot can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Littlefoot Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

## Trivia
- Littlefoot's appearance is based on the [[Yeti]], using a modified version of the skin.
- The item lore of [[Littlefoot Fluff]] states that Littlefoot is "Bigfoot's younger, lesser-known brother."

- Despite being a [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] [[Mob]] exclusive to the [[Glacite Mineshafts]], killing Littlefoot does not count towards the [[Commissions|Maniac Slayer]] commission.

# Livid
Aliases: Livid Enderman Clone
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Boss Collections
[Livid]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 430-982
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor V]]
Special Behavior: Creates 8 copies of himself
Description: Master Necromancer; Strongly believes he will become the Lord one day. Subject of mockeries, even from his disciples.
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 7,000,000-600,000,000; [[Defense]]: 100; [[Damage]]: 9,360-216,000
[Drops]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 1290-2946
[Metadata]
ID: LIVID_1; LIVID_CLONE_1; MASTER_LIVID_1; MASTER_LIVID_CLONE_1
Livid is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
## Collection
Killing Livid increases the Livid collection.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Dark Orb]] Reward|+15|
|II|100|Golden Livid Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|III|150|[[Livid Dagger]] Reward|+15|
|IV|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|V|500|[[Last Breath]] Reward|+25|
|VI|750|Shadow Assassin Chestplate ([[Shadow Assassin Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VII|1,000|Diamond Livid Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Boss Fight
### Overview
The remains of Thorn
Livid reveals a Dark Orb in the center of the arena as the remains of [[Thorn]] and that he is using its power to clone himself. After 16s, he blinds everyone for 1s and spawns eight clones from Endermen. Each of these clones has the same amount of health and stats, but they have different nametag colors. The real Livid can be identified by looking at the color of the ceiling once the clones have spawned.
When players kill a clone, it will regenerate all the way back to full HP and heal nearby clones by 25% of their maximum health. In [[Catacombs|Master Mode]], all Livids will also gain a [[Damage]] boost when a clone dies. Like Thorn, Livid has only one phase, which ends when the real Livid is killed (causing all clones to despawn).
Unlike other bosses, the boss bar does not show Livid's remaining [[Health]], so players cannot determine which entity is him by damaging them separately.
### Clones
The Livid Enderman clone
Each of the clones has a unique combat trait.
|Name|Trait|
|-|-|
|Vendetta Livid|Can teleport around and throw a Dagger at players.|
|Crossed Livid|Grants the Blindness effect to his target.|
|Hockey Livid|Charges at players, dealing damage.|
|Doctor Livid|Can throw a Splash Potion of Harming, dealing True Damage.|
|Frog Livid|Can poison players.|
|Smile Livid|Can throw a Splash Potion of Slowness.|
|Scream Livid|Can emit a knockback circle that deals True Damage and knocks back anyone in the vicinity.|
|Purple Livid|Leeches HP from targets.|
|Arcade Livid|Moves strangely and can shoot players with a bow.|
### Master Mode Changes
- Each Livid has 600,000,000 [[Health]] and a base [[Damage]] of 216,000 .
- All Livids will also gain a [[Damage]] boost when a clone dies.

|If the players have won|[BOSS] [name]: Impossible! How did you figure out which one I was?! [BOSS] [name]: If you think this is the end, you are so wrong! [BOSS] [name]: My shadows are everywhere, THEY WILL FIND YOU!!|

## Lore
Livid lived on the street and surviving by stealing fruit from peddler's carts until he joined the Guild of Thieves. There, he became a member when only 15 years old. He received his first assassination contract at the age of 17, directly from the Guild's leader. While Livid was a very skilled thief, this was going to be his first murder, and he enjoyed it, seeing it as art. Over the years, he started to think it was too easy for him, and sought more challenging kills.
One night, he went to an [[Wilderness|Inn]] to murder someone. Sitting at a table, he noticed [[Necron|a mysterious man]] come in, which was Necron before fully withering. Livid noticed that he hid a small chest in a long black cape. Very soon after Necron came in, Livid noticed [[The Watcher|another man]] was following him and studying Necron for almost an hour. This was Lathrop, the man that becomes the Watcher. After Necron suddenly left his table and went upstairs, Livid decided to learn more about him. He sat down with Lathrop, pretending to be drunk. He learned that Necron was "a merchant from far away." Livid decided that he would kill Necron as he was a "perfect target."
Livid went to Necron's room from the outside and saw that he was sleeping, but there was no sign of the small chest. He readied [[Livid Dagger|his dagger]] to strike, when his concentration was halted by a sound coming from an already inspected empty corner. Livid knew that sound does not come from thin air, unless magic is involved. The body in the bed was gone, and Livid felt himself being cut in half with no time to react. This was Necron. The blade was pulled out, no blood flowed, and Livid couldn't feel his body anymore.
His killer, [[Necron]], then took his body with him to [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. Necron quickly noticed he was followed by [[The Watcher|Lathrop]], after questioning him, Necron decided to take Lathrop with him to [[The Wither King|his Master]]. Livid then became a Master Necromancer, training disciples, presumably for Necron's army. However his subjects were not loyal to him and mock him. The reasons for this are currently unknown.
## Trivia
- The Livid Clones each have references in them.
  - Vendetta Livid wears a Guy Fawkes Mask, a reference to Guy Fawkes in the Gunpowder Plot in 1605.
  - Frog Livid wears a mask that looks very similar to the meme, Pepe the Frog.
  - Scream Livid wears a mask that is inspired by the painting, "The Scream" by Edvard Munch.
  - Crossed Livid’s mask is inspired by Día de los Muertos.
  - Smile Livid is likely a reference to the YouTuber Dream.
  - Hockey Livid’s behavior and name are likely a reference to the infamous fights that are known to occur in professional hockey.
  - Arcade Livid's mask references the classic arcade game, Space Invaders.
  - Doctor Livid's mask is face mask used to protect from disease and keep environments sterile.
- Livid is the only human in the Blood Room that does not speak.
- Livid's shadows he was talking about in his defeat speech could be [[Shadow Assassin|Shadow Assassins]].
- Livid had an Opti Fine cape of the flag of India.
  - On February 5, 2021, Reddit user u/TeamSyringe purchased the cape.
- Livid holds a Shadow Fury or a Silent Death instead of Livid dagger, boss fight and watcher summoned.
  - This is contradictory of the fact he calls his swords “Daggers”.
- When Livid summoned as Undead is defeated, it doesn't add to the collection.
- Livid's quote, "Unlimited power!" is a quote Star Wars, used by Darth Sidious, most notably in Star Wars: Episode III Revenge of the Sith said shortly after Mace Windu loses his hand.

Locusts can spawn in the [[The Garden]], starting at [[The Garden|Garden V (5)]].

Locusts jump away from the player. Locusts can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]] or [[Lassos]].

ID: MAGES_GUARD_200
Mage Guard is an NPC that patrols around [[Scarleton]].
## Spawning
Mage Guards can be found patrolling [[Scarleton]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Behavior
If a player attacks the guard at least three times, the player will be attacked. When hostile, the Mage Guard has 50,000,000 [[Health]] and deals 20,000 [[Damage]].
Once a player is targeted, the guards will chase all the way even into the upstairs of a building. [[Slowness Potion|Slowness]] splash potions can drastically obstruct their movement, useful when the player has difficulty on accessing merchant NPCs.
In a Barbarian rescue mission, if a bell near Mage Guard rings, the guards will be temporarily distracted and won't throw players out of the venue.

[Metadata]
ID: MAGE_OUTLAW_200
The Mage Outlaw is a hostile [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that can be found in the [[Courtyard]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
The Mage Outlaw spawns periodically within the [[Courtyard]].
## Behavior
The Mage Outlaw has 4 attack patterns, and will switch between them. It cannot be damaged outside of its arena.
- The Mage Outlaw stands in the center of the Courtyard, and summons a floating End Crystal, which constantly zaps players in the Courtyard. While the End Crystal is active, players who attempt to leave the area will be teleported back. The End Crystal disappears after 11 seconds or after players have dealt 10,000,000 [[Damage]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] to the Mage Outlaw.
- The Mage Outlaw floats into the air, and gains a Shield that reflects all damage dealt to it, appearing as a sphere of blue particles around it. It can then do one of two attacks:
  - Periodically spawns [[Nether Bricks]] and [[Sea Lantern|Sea Lanterns]] below players and strikes the blocks with lightning 1 second later, dealing heavy [[Damage]] to the players standing on them.
  - Spawns a circle of [[Block of Redstone|Blocks of Redstone]] on the ground, as well as a dodecahedron made of particles around it. Both rapidly increase in size, before dealing [[Damage]] to players standing inside them.
- The Mage Outlaw spawns 2 [[Mage Outlaw|Summoned Skulls]], which float around the Mage Outlaw. The skulls have 200,000 [[Health]]. When summoning the skulls, the Mage Outlaw is Invulnerable. The Skulls will begin to move towards the Mage Outlaw over an 8 second period. If they are not killed before they come into contact with the Mage Outlaw, they will be replaced with a new Summoned Skull, which floats around the Mage Outlaw and zaps players with a beam, dealing 2,000 [[Damage]]. It has 500,000 [[Health]]. Summoned Skulls are still active while the Mage Outlaw is using other attacks.

## Trivia
- The Mage Outlaws were apparently exiled from [[Scarleton]] due to their desire for power.
- The Mage Outlaw has no invincibility frames making high damage summons very good against it. However. Necromancy damage doesn't count towards your own damage.
- The Bestiary Lore states that he ate too much [[Sulphur]].
- Mage Outlaw shares the same skin with the [[Krondor Necromancer]].

# Magma Boss
Categories: Mobs, Confirmations needed, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crimson Isle, Bosses
[Magma Boss]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 523
Spawn Location: [[Magma Chamber]]
Mob Type: Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]]) Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 160,000,000; [[Damage]]: 2,000
[Drops]
Drops: 50x [[Crimson Essence]] 20–35x [[Magma Cream]] 1x [[Compact Ooze]] 1x [[Rekindled Ember Fragment]] 0–2x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] 0–1x Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]]) 0–1x EPIC Magma Cube Pet ([[Magma Cube Pet]]) 0–1x Hot Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]]) 0–1x [[Fire Fury Staff]] 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]] 0–1x [[Molten Cube]] 0–1x [[Magma Urchin]] 0–1x LEGENDARY Magma Cube Pet ([[Magma Cube Pet]])
XP: +3,922 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1569
[Metadata]
ID: MAGMA_CUBE_BOSS_100
The Magma Boss is a [[Mob]] and [[Bosses|BOSS]] found in the [[Magma Chamber]] of the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
The Magma Boss spawns every 2 minutes at the center lava pool. The spawn time is identical in every server.
## Behavior
The Magma Boss has 3 states: normal, split, and fleeing.
The normal state occurs when the Magma Boss is not in any other states. The split state occurs every time the boss has taken 32M [[Damage]] (at 128M [[Health]] , 96M [[Health]] , 64M [[Health]] , 32M [[Health]] [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and 0M [[Health]] ). The flee state occurs after the last split.
During the normal state, the Magma Boss can do the following:
- Shoot a fireball at a nearby player every second, which deals [[Damage]] on hit.
- Create a circle of fire particles on the ground.
- Jump into the air and land in the circle of fire, crushing all players beneath.
  - Before landing, the Magma Boss launches 3 projectiles towards the opposite side of the arena. Upon touching the ground, they create large areas of fire particles which deal [[Damage]] to players standing in them every 0.5 seconds. They disappear before the Magma Boss jumps again.
The "Damage Soaked" by the Magma Boss can be seen as a meter on the scoreboard. It can alter its damage increase per cycle if filled and can be filled multiple times over with different effects and thresholds:
- Yellow is the basic requirement to enter the split phase. It increases the Magma Boss's damage by +20% [[Damage]].
- Orange is the second threshold. It increases damage by +25% [[Damage]].
- Red is the final threshold. It increases damage by +35% [[Damage]].
The Magma Boss always jumps at least 3 times before entering its split state.
During the split state, the Magma Boss jumps to the center of the arena and splits into many [[Magma Glare|Magma Glares]] and [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]].
- [[Magma Glare|Magma Glares]] target the nearest player and shoots fireballs. They can also deal [[Damage]] on contact. Each [[Magma Glare]] targeting a player increases the damage taken from them and [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]].
  - This can be calculated with DamageMultiplier=2*TargetingMagmaGlares. The maximum multiplier is 14.
- [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]] jump towards the player to deal [[Damage]] on hit.
The scoreboard will have the "Kill the Magmas" meter showing the amount of mobs required to kill. Once filled, the Magma Boss will return to its normal state. It will announce in chat the amount of [[Damage]] increase as it reforms.
When the Magma Boss reaches a cumulative +100% [[Damage]] or more, it will enter its final flee state. During this state, the Magma Boss shrinks and is set to 10M [[Health]]. It runs away from players while shooting fireballs. As its [[Health]] decreases, it moves faster and continues to shrink.

## Strategy
Ideally, multiple players should work together to efficiently defeat the boss. Since the player has a higher chance of receiving rare drops if they deal more damage than other players, many players attempt to maximize their DPS.
Bows are the best way to deal damage against the main boss, as well as being the safest. One of the most cost-effective setups to kill the Magma Boss consistently is using at least 2 pieces of [[Terror Armor]] and a [[Juju Shortbow]] as Hydra Strikes can damage the boss very effectively. The late game bow, the [[Terminator]] does an even better job and can consistently deal maximum damage per Normal phase to reach the max damage threshold. This allows a solo player to defeat the boss at the fastest time indistinguishable from cooperative play due to the aforementioned mandatory three jumps from the boss per cycle.
Note that [[Arrows]] can often bounce off the Magma Boss due to Minecraft mechanics.
Magic weapons can be used to deal damage during the Normal phase, but are not as effective as shortbows even if using a [[Hyperion]] with powerful armor. It is highly effective in clearing out mobs during the split and fleeing phase though.
Using a melee weapon is a much riskier strategy of damaging the boss, due to it being commonly guarded by the AOE attacks, and is harder to hit. Melee builds are also nearly useless during the Split phase as the magma cubes especially the Unstable Magma will certainly kill the player. It is not advised to undertake this strategy unless they have very effective armor such as [[Necron's Armor]] or [[Crimson Armor]]. If the player has a good bow, melee is not recommended outright.
When the boss splits into smaller cubes, players should go after the Glares, since they are the ones responsible for the massive multiplicative damage. If using the [[Hyperion]], try using Wither Impact above them instead of directly towards them to reduce the chances of teleporting into them.
When entering its Fleeing phase, it will always first start moving diagonally towards negative-X and negative-Z (towards the [[Hellwisp]] area). Use the time when it's reforming to camp the spot as mage or to aim towards it as an archer to immediately kill it.
When the boss dies, each player gets separate drops. If a player quits the lobby, gets limboed, or exits the game when the items are dropped, they will not receive the items.
## Trivia
- The Magma Boss was the first boss added and revamped from an early-game boss to a late-game boss.
- The [[Ember Rod]] used to be a drop from the Magma Boss but was later removed.
  - [[Ember Fragment|Ember Fragments]] also used to drop from the Magma Boss, but have since been replaced with [[Rekindled Ember Fragment|Rekindled Ember Fragments]].
- Contrary to popular belief, the player is not required to land the killing blow to have the Magma Boss count towards their [[Bestiary]].
- While an [[Aurora Staff]] does not work on the main boss, it does on [[Magma Glare|Magma Glares]] and [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]].
- The Magma Boss is not affected by [[Derpy|Derpy's]] double HP perk during its normal and fleeing states.
  - The [[Magma Glare|Magma Glares]] and [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]], however, are.
## Bugs
- The Magma Boss occasionally gets stuck during the flee state, becoming immobile and invulnerable.
  - This can possibly happen if the chunks are unloaded during the final split state.
  - This can be fixed by pushing it into the lava so it goes through blocks to take suffocation damage.
  - No loot will appear on its death if it stays in this bugged state for too long.

The Magma Cube is a [[Mob]] that can be summoned by setting a [[Slime (Private Island)|Slime]] on fire.
## Spawning
[[Slime (Private Island)|Slimes]] can be turned into [Lv7] Magma Cubes by lighting them on fire. This can be done by using a [[Lava Bucket]], [[Flint and Steel]], [[Burning Potion]] ( [[God Potion]] also works) or a weapon with the [[Fire Aspect]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]].
## Behavior
[Lv3] Magma Cubes jump towards the player and attempt to hit them, dealing [[Damage]]. When killed, they do not split into smaller Magma Cubes.

# Magma Glare
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Crimson Isle
[Magma Glare]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 195
Spawn Location: [[Magma Chamber]]
Mob Type: Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]]) Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 2,000,000; [[Damage]]: 2,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]]
XP: +292 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 214
[Metadata]
ID: MAGMA_GLARE_100
Magma Glare is a [[Magma Cube]] type [[Mob]] that spawns when fighting [[Magma Boss]].
## Spawning
After each phase of [[Magma Boss]], it hides and spawns [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]] and Magma Glares. After each phase, 2 [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] Magma Glares spawn targeting each player that has attacked the boss. On top of that, up to 5 additional Magma Glares will spawn, depending on the [[Damage]] dealt by the player.[Confirm]
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv195]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Cyclamen Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.00004%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv195]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|65|65|65|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|292|292|292|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|214|214|214|
## Behavior
After spawned Magma Glares slowly bounce towards their respective players, attacking them with ranged attacks. They deal 2000 [[Damage]] per hit, which is increases based on the number of Magma Glares targeting the hit player via the following formula (2*n)*1000 where n is the number of Magma Glares. Their damage bonus and the amount of Magma Glares targeting the player is shown in their action bar.
After enough of them are killed, both [[Unstable Magma|Unstable Magmas]] and Magma Glares drift towards the center of arena and [[Magma Boss]] is once again spawned.

|Jul 15, 2026|Changed the exponential scaling of the [[Damage]] dealt (2^n) to use linear scaling instead (2*n).|
|Aug 25, 2026|Changed the Mob Level ([[Mob]]) from [Lv100] to [Lv195], [[Coins]] from 125 to 65, [[Experience]] from 200 to 214, and [[Combat]] XP from 0 to 292.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

[Drops]
Drops: 48x [[Ancient Claw]] 0–48x [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 0–4x [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 0–1x [[Fateful Stinger]] 0–1x [[Mythological Dye]] 0–1x [[Manti-core]]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 414
[Metadata]
ID: MANTICORE_1750
A Manticore is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Manticore has a chance to spawn when digging [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
|Griffin Pet Rarity|Prefix|Level|Stats|Spawn Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Empyrean|[Lv1,750]|125,000,000 [[Health]]; 5,000 [[Damage]]|0.26%; (1 in 385.33 mobs)|
[[Tracking]] increases the chance to spawn Manticore.
## Behavior
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Manticore mainly attacks using contact damage with its high speed.
Occasionally[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], the Manticore uses its Fatal Sting attack. Green particles are emitted from the Manticore's head for a few seconds before shooting a barrage of arrows at the player. If any arrows hit a player, this deals immense damage to the player, disables all healing effects, and starts a timer, requiring the player to defeat the Manticore before the timer runs out. Failing to do so instantly kills the player. This attack can be dodged with enough speed and distance. When a player is hit, the following dialogue appears in chat:
FATAL STING! You were fatally stung by a Manticore! Kill it before the time runs out or die!
Attempting to heal after getting hit causes the following dialogue to appear in chat:
The Fatal Sting of the Manticore disabled all healing!
The Manticore is immune to the stunning effect of [[Fire Freeze Staff]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv377]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|

# Maxor
Aliases: Energy Crystal, Energy Crystals, Wither Guard (Catacombs)
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Dungeon Mobs
[Maxor]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 575-1,125
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Description: The Wither Lords; Disciples of the Wither King. Inherited the Catacombs eons ago. Never defeated, feared by anything living AND dead.
Mob Type: [[Wither]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 100,000,000-800,000,000; [[Defense]]: 1,000 (Master Mode); [[Damage]]: 7,200-720,000
[Metadata]
ID: MAXOR_1; MASTER_MAXOR_1
Maxor is a [[Bosses|BOSS]] along with [[Storm]], [[Goldor]] and [[Necron]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] and is a disciple of [[The Wither King]].
## Collection
Killing Maxor and ALL other Withers increases the Necron collection.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Wither Blood]] Reward|+15|
|II|100|Golden Necron Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|III|150|Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+15|
|IV|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|V|500|Wither Leggings ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VI|750|Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VII|1,000|Diamond Necron Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
## Boss Fight
The boss fight is split into four phases (Five phases in Master Mode). There are four Wither Lords: Maxor, [[Storm]], [[Goldor]] and [[Necron]]. Each Wither will be a boss for a phase that must be killed individually for each phase.
### Phase 1
Maxor's fight begins in a factory-like arena. Once the fight begins, large numbers of Wither Miners and Guards appear in the arena. He will be invulnerable. Players must retrieve two Energy Crystals from high ledges and place them on the podiums at the sides of the arena. This activates a laser at the back of the arena, which Maxor must be lured into. This process must be done at least twice. The first time Maxor is in the laser, he will be vulnerable until losing 75% of his [[Health]]. After that, Maxor becomes enraged and invulnerable again. The second time or later, Maxor becomes vulnerable, but doesn't have a damage cap. Once Maxor dies, the players will progress into the next phase.

|[Lv285-609] [[Wither Guard]]|[[Health]]: 3,500,000-300,000,000; [[Damage]]: 18,000-202,500|Can shoot players from extremely high range with a bow, which has high knockback.|[[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]|[[Skeletal]] (Normal Only) [[Wither]] Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) (MM Only)|0–1x [[Bone Dye]]|427-913 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 95–203 [[Coins]]; 313-669 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv283-557] [[Wither Miner]]|[[Health]]: 10,000,000-300,000,000; [[Damage]]: 6,000-80,000|Can run around the arena, dealing damage with high knockback.|[[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]|[[Skeletal]] (Normal Only) [[Wither]] Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) (MM Only)|0–1x [[Bone Dye]]|424-835 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 94–185 [[Coins]]; 311-612 [[Experience]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|When Phase 1 starts|[BOSS] Maxor: WELL WELL WELL LOOK WHO'S HERE! [BOSS] Maxor: I'VE BEEN TOLD I COULD HAVE A BIT OF FUN WITH YOU. [BOSS] Maxor: DON'T DISAPPOINT ME, I HAVEN'T HAD A GOOD FIGHT IN A WHILE.|
|When led into the laser|[BOSS] Maxor: YOU TRICKED ME! [BOSS] Maxor: THAT BEAM! IT HURTS! IT HURTS!!|
|When killed|[BOSS] Maxor: I'M TOO YOUNG TO DIE AGAIN! [BOSS] Maxor: I'LL MAKE YOU REMEMBER MY DEATH!!|
|If all players die|[BOSS] Maxor: FINALLY! This took way too long. [BOSS] Maxor: Now that you're a Ghost, can you help me clean up?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[BOSS] Maxor: YOUR WEAPONS CAN'T PIERCE THROUGH MY SHIELD!|
|None|[BOSS] Maxor: YOUR MOBILITY TRICKS DON'T WORK IN MY DOMAIN!|
|None|[BOSS] Maxor: I HOPE YOU LIKE EXPLOSIONS TOO!|
|None|[BOSS] Maxor: MY MINIONS WILL HAVE TO WIPE THE FLOOR AFTER I'M DONE WITH YOU ALL!|
|When firing Wither Skulls|[BOSS] Maxor: How about you taste some rapid fire Wither Skulls!|
|When firing Wither Skulls|[BOSS] Maxor: Eat Wither Skulls, scum!|
|When firing Wither Skulls|[BOSS] Maxor: Time for me to blast you away for good!|
## Trivia
- Maxor was going to be the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] before Hypixel removed Maxor, [[Goldor]] and [[Storm]] from the game and replaced them with [[Necron]].
  - All three bosses were later re-added to The Catacombs - Floor VII.
- [[Maxor's Armor]] and [[Maxor the Fish]] are references to this boss.
- Starting in 1.21.5 and beyond, Maxor, Storm, & Necron will now appear with normal textures of the Wither.
- In 2020, a video was posted by Jayavarmen of the original Goldor fight.

A Miner Skeleton is a [[Mob]] found in the [[Deep Caverns]] within the [[Diamond Reserve]] and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]].
## Spawning
Lower level Miner Skeletons can be found in the [[Diamond Reserve]], while higher level ones can be found in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]].

XP: +22 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 16
[Metadata]
ID: DIAMOND_ZOMBIE_20
A Miner Zombie is a [[Mob]] found in the [[Deep Caverns]]. The lower level version is found in the [[Diamond Reserve]], while the higher level one spawns in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]. They are also fireproof.
## Spawning
Lower level Miner Zombies can be found in the [[Diamond Reserve]], while higher level ones can be found in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]].

|[Lv15]|[[Obsidian Sanctuary]]|300|250|22|16|5|DIAMOND_ZOMBIE_20|
## Behavior
A Miner Zombie attacks the player like a normal [[Zombie]].

Miner Zombie can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Miner Zombie Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+750 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Tips
- Miner Zombies drop [[Miner Armor]], a good early game defense armor to mine the ores that they protect.
- While they take no damage from fire or lava, Miner Zombies are still slowed in lava.
## Trivia
- Miner Zombies in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] drop [[Miner Armor]] with [[Enchantments|Protection V]].
  - The Miner Zombies in the [[Diamond Reserve]] drop [[Miner Armor]] with no enchants.

Mushroom Bull is a hostile [[Mob]] found in the [[Mystic Marsh]].
## Behavior
Mushroom Bulls are hostile mobs that run quickly (sometimes in circles) and attack players with melee strikes. They graze [[Mushroom Spore|Mushroom Spores]] when walking over them (without healing). After consuming spores, their deaths trigger nearby [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]] explosions, similarly to the effect after consuming [[Marsh Spore Soup]].

# Mutant Tarantula
Aliases: Tarantula Mutant
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Slayer, Spider Slayer, Minibosses
[Mutant Tarantula]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: SLAYER MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 251
Spawn Condition: Tier IV [[Spider Slayer]]
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 750,000; [[Damage]]: 2,000
[Drops]
Drops: 5x [[Tarantula Web]]
XP: +1,882 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 753
[Metadata]
ID: SPIDER_FOLLOWER_4_BOOSTED
The Mutant Tarantula is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSS]] which spawns very rarely when a player doing the [[Tarantula Broodfather]] Tier IV quest kills a spider.
## Spawning
Mutant Tarantulas spawn rarely during [[Tarantula Broodfather]] Tier IV quests when the player kills a Spider-type mob. When this happens, an explosion effect takes place and the Mutant Tarantula spawns.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv251]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Tarantula Web]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv251]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|1,882|1,882|1,882|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|753|753|753|
## Behavior
The Mutant Tarantula has 576k [[Health]] . It seeks out and attacks the nearest player, preferring the player who spawned it.
It moves more slowly than its cousin the [[Tarantula Beast]]. However, it is far more deadly, dealing 5,000 damage per hit - enough to kill players wearing maxed out [[Tarantula Armor]] within 5 hits.
## Tips
- A particular strategy to beating this miniboss is to gain lower ground; standing on a ledge 2 blocks beneath the miniboss, will generally make it land no hits against the player, and with virtually any decent late game sword, such as a [[Scorpion Foil]], one can take down a Mutant Tarantula.
- Players can also lure the miniboss to a place far enough for the AI of the miniboss to stop working. This will make the miniboss not be aggravated and one can easily kill it down with no risk.

# Necron
Aliases: Necron's Apostle
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, More info needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Dungeon Mobs, Boss Collections
[Necron]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 739-1,094
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Description: The Wither Lords; Disciples of the Wither King. Inherited the Catacombs eons ago. Never defeated, feared by anything living AND dead.
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]; [[Wither]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000-1,400,000,000; [[Defense]]: 2,100 (Master Mode); [[Damage]]: 7,200-300,000
[Metadata]
ID: NECRON_1; MASTER_NECRON_1
Necron is a [[Bosses|BOSS]] along with [[Maxor]], [[Storm]] and [[Goldor]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] and is a disciple of [[The Wither King]].
## Collection
Killing Necron and the other Withers increases the Necron collection.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Wither Blood]] Reward|+15|
|II|100|Golden Necron Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|III|150|Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+15|
|IV|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|V|500|Wither Leggings ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VI|750|Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VII|1,000|Diamond Necron Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Boss fight
The boss fight is split into four phases. (Five phases in Master Mode.) There are four Wither Lords: Maxor, Storm, Goldor, and Necron. Each Wither will be a boss for a phase that must be killed individually for each phase.
Necron has 1,000,000,000 [[Health]] or 1,400,000,000 [[Health]] in Master Mode. He sometimes has a shield surrounding him, which protects him from all attacks.
In all parts of the boss fight, lava damages the player for 20% of their max health, as well as launching them upwards in one of 2 ways:
- Regular: You are launched into the air at a standard height ([More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) blocks).
- Super: Looking upwards at a pitch of -40 or lower triggers a Super lava bounce, boosting you 30% higher than a Regular jump.
### Phase 4
The trapdoor in the core entrance will open after Goldor is defeated. As the players are about to reach the bottom layer of the factory, Necron hovers the players in the air over a platform surrounded by a ring of lava when he gives his welcome speech. He destroys the platform beneath the players and releases them into the lava.
At 80% [[Health]], he will teleport to the center platform, blind the players, and begin a frenzy attack, dealing even more damage. The radius of this frenzy increases over time and decreases with the damage dealt. When it reaches 0, Necron will destroy a section of the outer ring and then resume attacking the players. This takes 10 seconds. This repeats at 5% [[Health]].
The fight finishes when Necron reaches 0 [[Health]].
Necron has several attacks.
- Summoning a barrage of wither skulls launched from each of his three heads. They do high [[Damage]] and are fired rapidly, dealing high knockback.
  - Necron will sometimes stay still and fire wither skulls at a much faster rate.
- He enters a frenzy where explosions are centered on him, dealing extremely high amounts of damage to any player who gets close. The size of the explosions will increase while Necron is not being damaged and decrease when Necron is.
- Shooting fireballs at players.
- Causing lightning to strike nearby.
- Summoning four floating Diamond Swords and launching them towards players, dealing [[Damage]] and knockback.
- He flies into the air and summons four horizontal groups of beams, rotating around Necron for 15s. They deal 25% of the player's [[Health]] in [[True Damage]] on contact.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|When Phase 4 starts; (If the player has not defeated Necron before)|[BOSS] Necron: Finally, I heard so much about you. The Eye likes you very much. [BOSS] Necron: You went further than any human before, congratulations. [BOSS] Necron: I'm afraid, your journey ends now. [BOSS] Necron: My master and I spent centuries building this factory...and this army. [BOSS] Necron: I won't allow you to destroy it all now. [BOSS] Necron: Goodbye.|
|When Phase 4 starts; (If the player has defeated Necron before)|[BOSS] Necron: You went further than any human before, congratulations. [BOSS] Necron: I'm afraid, your journey ends now. [BOSS] Necron: Goodbye.|
|After the first platform is destroyed|[BOSS] Necron: That's a very impressive trick. I guess I'll have to handle this myself.|
|While attacking with Swords|[BOSS] Necron: I ensure you, these blades are far sharper than Goldor's ever were.|
|When killed|[BOSS] Necron: All this, for nothing... [BOSS] Necron: I understand your words now, my master. [BOSS] Necron: The Catacombs... are no more.|
|If all players die|[BOSS] Necron: FINALLY! This took way too long. [BOSS] Necron: Now that you're a Ghost, can you help me clean up?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[BOSS] Necron: You merely adopted the Catacombs! I was molded by them!|
|None|[BOSS] Necron: I - Necron - was destined to rule over Mankind! Dead or Alive!|
|None|[BOSS] Necron: The Catacombs made you stronger, but can you beat me?!|
|None|[BOSS] Necron: Show me how you beat Storm!!!|
|None|[BOSS] Necron: Not just your Village, but the whole world will end!|
|None|[BOSS] Necron: Fight for your life!|
|Before entering frenzy|[BOSS] Necron: Sometimes when you have a problem, you just need to destroy it all and start again.|
|Before entering frenzy|[BOSS] Necron: WITNESS MY RAW NUCLEAR POWER!|
|While in frenzy|[BOSS] Necron: IF YOU WANT TO STAY COOL, DON'T LOOK!|
|While in frenzy|[BOSS] Necron: BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOM|
|While in frenzy|[BOSS] Necron: HASTA LA VISTA, BABY!|
|When frenzy fails|[BOSS] Necron: ARGH!|
## Lore
- Necron, similarly to other inhabitants of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], was a human. After being orphaned by the [[Jythar|Calamity]], Necron sought to discover what magic killed his parents. That is why he joined the Mage's Academy. Unfortunately, he was not very good; he was even called "the worst student," despite being one of the oldest students at 17 years old. He desperately tried gaining access to Scrolls with forbidden magic, knowing that they were the answer to his problems, but his requests were always denied.
- Ten days before the mage exam that Necron, with his current knowledge, did not have a chance to pass, he sat in his room, unable to sleep. He cursed the dean of the association for restricting his access to knowledge when he suddenly heard a subtle noise - a rustle of paper. Someone has slid some paper below his door. He could not believe what he saw, he quickly opened the door - but there was nobody there. Necron was holding a scroll with forbidden magic. He did not keep his interest in them a secret, but the scrolls were protected with advanced spells. Stealing them was unthinkable to anyone. He started learning the spell on the scroll. The scroll was able to make him invisible to regular magic. This spell would make him immune to vision spells and able to bypass weaker barriers - he thought of only one place where that would be useful, and he had no intention of going there.
- Over a couple of days, he mastered the spell and thought of an almost too-perfect way to use it. He knew that for over a decade after his defeat, [[The Wither King]] was imprisoned in [[Wilderness|a cage]]. He was guarded by mages and a barrier, but the guards preferred to stay away from the cage itself. Necron pretended to help the guards with their chores. Despite his immense stress, he cast the spell in the forest, walked to the barrier, and crossed it without problems. As he approached the cage behind the barrier, he noticed the ground and everything around him being corrupted, losing its color. He saw it, a wither creature, about twice his size. He did it. He met Kaeman, The Wither King.
- Only the middle of his heads seemed to notice him; "You are using my spell," the Wither said. He was surprised to hear the voice, and he heard it again. "Who are you, and why are you here?" it said. "Why am I here?" Necron finally answered, "My name is Necron. You killed my parents 17 years ago…" After a brief silence, The Wither King responded, "Is that why you are here, Necron?" "No..." Necron answered. He proceeded to question Kaeman about him, and the [[Fels|Fel Magic]] Apart from their common negative opinion of the Mage's Academy, they found more similarities within each other. Kaeman offered to teach Necron the basics of Dark Magic. Necron quickly lost interest in revenge against Kaeman.
- Sometime later, Kaeman wanted something in return for being Necron's master. He wished to escape. They grew close, so Necron did not hesitate. Kaeman prepared a spell, and it was time. While it only lasted a few seconds, it was the most complicated thing Necron ever had to conjure. After the barrier was destabilized, Kaeman broke his cage and a part of the island, then jumped to the Void together with Necron, stopping their fall after about 100 meters, entering an opening in the cliff, and sealing it immediately behind them. "Just what is… What is this place?" Necron explained. "The Catacombs," Kaeman replied.
- Five years after the events of the Journals Apprentice, [[Journal|Part 1]]; [[Journal|Part 2]] and [[Journal|Part 3]], Kaeman taught Necron some more until he became a powerful mage. One night, he went to [[Wilderness|an Inn]] for an unknown reason, where a young murderer attempted on his life. Necron took [[Livid]]'s corpse to The Catacombs, but he was followed. From his follower, [[The Watcher|Lathrop]], he learned that the reason for following a stranger was his visions, which told Lathrop that following him to his master would make him meet his full potential. Necron took his follower with him, and not long after taking him to Kaeman, Lathrop became [[The Watcher]].
- After [[The Watcher]]'s transformation, he was asked by The Wither King to create a world where he would live his happy days again. After difficult and exhausting work, he succeeded. The Wither King secluded himself from The Catacombs, entrusted Necron to be in charge of The Catacombs, and put The Watcher in Necron's service.
- After destroying the Expedition to the Catacombs and sending [[Storm]] to destroy the castle. He began questioning his desire for conquest.
## Trivia
- Necron smote and killed [[The Professor]], as shown at the end of [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] boss fight. This was his first introduction and was released back when he was the final boss of Dungeons.
- Necron, [[Storm]], [[Goldor]] and [[Dante]] are the only mobs who can destroy constructions.
- Back when he was the boss on Floor 7 alone, he had most phases of any existing boss - 4. These phases now feature other Wither Lords.
- One of his old quotes, "YOU MERELY ADOPTED [[Catacombs|THE CATACOMBS]], I WAS MOLDED BY THEM!" is a reference from the film The Dark Knight Rises.
  - This line is also used by [[Paul]] when he is a candidate for the mayor election.
- The "HASTA LA VISTA, BABY!" quote is a reference to The Terminator 2 and its subsequent sequels, where the catchphrase is referenced.
- The name Necron comes from the Ancient Greek word for "dead" or "the dead" - νεκρός (nekrós). In this context, however, it could be interpreted as “of death” or “corpse”.
- Necron and [[The Watcher]] are similarly old, both orphaned by someone using [[Fels|Fel Magic]]. While The Watcher's parents were killed by [[Jythar|The Calamity]], it is never specified whether Necron's were killed by it too, or by [[The Wither King]] using the same magic.
- The description of [[Sorrow]] implies that those who go through immense distress become Ghosts; however, neither he nor [[The Wither King|Kaeman]] did become Ghosts.
- Necron's beam attack is closely similar to Broken Heart Radiation used by a [[Voidgloom Seraph]] Tier IV, albeit without a debuff of decreased [[Vitality]].
- Necron is the only Wither Lord that is completely vulnerable to damage throughout his boss fight. Maxor, Storm, and Goldor will only become vulnerable when a certain variable is met.

Nest Endermite is a mob that spawns in [[The End]] and has a chance to spawn when mining an [[Ender Nodes|Ender Node]].
## Spawning
Nest Endermite can spawn in groups of 2 to 4, or larger groups when influenced by the [[Endermite Pet]]. They have a 33.21% to spawn from an [[End Stone]] [[Ender Nodes|Ender Node]], and a 30.54% to spawn from an [[Obsidian]] [[Ender Nodes|Ender Node]].
## Behavior
Nest Endermites crawls toward the player to deal [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]].

Obsidian Defenders are hostile mobs found exclusively in the [[Dragon's Nest]].
## Behavior
Obsidian Defenders are hostile, inflict the Wither potion effect, and are extremely fast. They are also completely immune to all knockback unless the player is wielding a weapon with the Knockback enchantment, from which they would take significant knockback.
They are Wither Skeletons that wear a block of Obsidian on their heads, along with an [[Obsidian Chestplate]].

# Odonata
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Mob pages missing internal IDs, Rift Dimension
[Odonata]
Type: [[Mob]]
Spawn Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Mob Type: None
An Odonata is a [[Mob]] in the [[Wyld Woods]]. Odonatas can be found floating between tree branches aimlessly. Since they will generally be too far to bottle from the tree branches, [[Larva Silk]] and a [[Silkwire Stick]] will be required to bottle an Odonata.
## Drops
The player can hold an [[Empty Odonata Bottle]] in a 3 block radius of an Odonata whilst looking at it for a few seconds to transform the item into a [[Bottled Odonata]].
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Odonata.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

Omegagmas spawn from [[Magma Follower|Magma Followers]] that merge together in the [[Kuudra]] fight.

# Oubliette Guard
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
[Oubliette Guard]

Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Oubliette]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 60; [[Rift Damage]]: 5
[Drops]
XP: +1 Combat XP ([[Combat]])

The Oubliette Guard is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Oubliette]].
## Drops
Non-item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Motes]]|10|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|1|
## Behavior
Oubliette Guards are passive until a player damages them. When hostile, they move towards the player and take 5 [[Rift Time]] from the player on contact. Once all Oubliette Guards are defeated, a message will appear in chat, saying:
All the guards have been defeated, you are free to go!
The entrance to the [[Oubliette]] will then fall, and allow the player to escape.

The Pack Magma Cube is a [[Mob]] that spawns on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Pack Magma Cubes spawn naturally in the caves north and south of the [[Magma Chamber]].
## Behavior
Upon being hit for the first time, Pack Magma Cubes will sometimes become 2 Pack Magma Cubes. They will attempt to jump towards the player and attack them, dealing 4000 [[Damage]] on hit.
