|[[Field Mouse]]|Random|5|||[[Squeaky Toy]] [[Squeaky Mousemat]]|[[Field Mouse Shard]]|
## Spawning
Pests are able to spawn on [[The Garden]] in a few ways starting at [[The Garden|Garden V (5)]].
### Farming
Pests are able to spawn while a [[Player]] is actively farming. Pests have a base 0.2% (1 in 500) chance to spawn per crop broken while off cooldown. The chance can be increased by up to 200% using [[Salty Sprayonator]], and 18% using the [[Atmospheric Filter]] during Autumn with [[Attributes|Filter Upgrade X]], making the highest possible chance 0.636% (1 in 157.23) during Autumn or 0.6% (1 in 166.67) outside of Autumn.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
Pests may spawn in the plot the player is farming in or in any of the 4 adjacent plots, including default plots and [[Greenhouse]] plots, but excluding locked plots, void plots, and the Barn plot. Offline Pests can still spawn on the Barn plot with the use of [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]].
#### Bonus Pest Chance
By default, only one Pest will spawn at a time. This can be increased via [[Bonus Pest Chance]].
#### Spawn Cooldown
After Pests spawn, spawning goes on cooldown for a base 5 minutes. The cooldown can be modified from a few sources. All percentage modifiers stack additively.
|Source|Modifier|
|-|-|
|[[Finnegan]] (Pest Eradicator)|-20%|
|[[Pesthunter's Necklace]]|-10%|
|[[Pesthunter's Cloak]]|-10%|
|[[Pest Vest]]|-15%|
|[[Pesthunter's Belt]]|-10%|
|[[Pesthunter's Gloves]]|-10%|
|Squeaky ([[Reforging]]) on [[Equipment]]|-2.5% each|
|Pest Cooldown ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Moth Shard]])|up to -5s|
|[[Pest Repellent]]|2x|
|[[Pest Repellent MAX]]|4x|
The minimum achievable cooldown is 1 minutes 10 seconds with Pest Eradicator and 2 minutes 10 seconds without, while the maximum is 20 minutes.
[[Pest Repellent]] and [[Pest Repellent MAX]] do not stack, with the latter taking priority.
#### Attracting Specific Pests
By default, each Pest has a weight of 100, meaning they have an equal chance to be chosen whenever one spawns. The weights for specific pests can be modified in several ways.
|Method|Effect|
|-|-|
|[[Sprayonator]], [[Juicy Sprayonator]], or [[Salty Sprayonator]]|Increases the weight of targeted Pests by 1,000.|
|[[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] (Stereo Harmony)|Increases the weight of the targeted Pest by 1,000.|
|[[Mosquito Pet]] (Smooth Jazz)|Increases the effectiveness of Stereo Harmony by 0.5%–50% (bringing its weight increase to 1,005–1,500).|
### Offline
While no players are present and the [[The Garden]] is unloaded, Offline Pests are able to spawn at a rate of 1 Pest per 1 Hour.
#### Disco
[[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]] can be placed on [[The Garden]] to force all offline Pests to spawn within 10 blocks of it. It requires each Pest's corresponding [[Pest Vinyls|Pest Vinyl]] to attract it. Only one [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]] can be placed at a time.
### Traps
If the player has [[Pest Trap|Pest Traps]], [[Mouse Trap|Mouse Traps]], or [[Vermin Trap|Vermin Traps]] placed down with [[Sprayonator]] material inside, Pests can spawn in them. Each Pest that spawns in a trap consumes 1 [[Sprayonator]] material. Whether the player is online or offline, the spawn rate is about 1 Pest per 15 Minutes.
#### Field Mouse
A [[Field Mouse]] can spawn in a [[Pest Trap]] with a base chance of 2.78% (1 in 36), a [[Mouse Trap]] with a base chance of 7.89% (1 in 12.67), or a [[Vermin Trap]] with a base chance of 10.26% (1 in 9.75). The chance for a Field Mouse is also affected by [[Tracking]].
#### Lunar Moth
A [[Lunar Moth]] is guaranteed to spawn in any trap with [[Moondew]].
## Behavior
Most pests will attempt to flee from the player, but they also try to stay in the plot they spawned in, with the exception of the [[Earthworm]] pest who will not try to flee from the player.
When 4 or more Pests are on [[The Garden]] at once, the player's [[Farming Fortune]] (as well as each crop-specific fortune stat) is reduced, first by 5% and then multiples of 15%. [[Farming Fortune]] will continue to be lost for each additional Pest, up to 8, after which no more Pests will spawn until at least one is killed. The maximum [[Farming Fortune]] loss is 75%.
For each 100 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] the player has, they can have 1 more Pest on their Garden before their [[Farming Fortune]] starts being reduced. The reduction then starts at 5% and scales from there.
Effect of number of Pests on [[Farming Fortune]]:
|Pests|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 0-99 [[Bonus Pest Chance]]|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 100-199 [[Bonus Pest Chance]]|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 200-299 [[Bonus Pest Chance]]|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 300-399 [[Bonus Pest Chance]]|[[Farming Fortune]] Reduction 400-499 [[Bonus Pest Chance]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|0||||||
|1||||||
|2||||||
|3||||||
|4|5%|||||
|5|15%|5%||||
|6|30%|15%|5%|||
|7|50%|30%|15%|5%||
|8|75%|50%|30%|15%|5%|
## Game Message
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
Table:
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|1 Pest spawned|GROSS! A Pest has appeared in Plot - [name]! CLICK HERE to teleport to the plot!|
|2 Pests spawned|EWW! 2 Pest have spawned in Plot - [name]! CLICK HERE to teleport to the plot!|
|3 or more Pests spawned|YUCK! # Pest have spawned in Plot - [name]! CLICK HERE to teleport to the plot!|
|Farming Fortune reduction|Farming Fortune has been reduced by #%!|
|Offline Pest spawn|GROSS! While you were offline, Pests spawned in Plots [plot], [plot] and [plot]!|
|Trying to damage a spawned Pest|You can only damage Pests with a Vacuum! Visit SkyMart to obtain one!|
## Uses
[[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] can be exchanged at [[Pesthunter Phillip]] to gain temporary 5 [[Farming Fortune]] for 30 minutes per consumed Pest, up to 40 [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]]. They can also be exchanged for various items in [[Pesthunter Phillip]]'s shop.
## Drops
### Guaranteed Drops
Each Pest drops a specific number of its respective crop, this number is affected by [[Farming Fortune]] and the crop's respective [[Crop Fortune]].
Table:

|[[Pumpkin]]|1 [[Enchanted Pumpkin]]|+1 every 52.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|49 [[Enchanted Pumpkin]]|78,400 [[Coins]]|

|[[Sugar Cane]]|2 [[Enchanted Sugar]]|+1 every 26.25 [[Farming Fortune]]|98 [[Enchanted Sugar]]|62,720 [[Coins]]|

|[[Potato]]|3 [[Enchanted Potato]]|+1 every 15.75 [[Farming Fortune]]|163 [[Enchanted Potato]]|78,240 [[Coins]]|

[[Field Mouse]] always drops one of these crops. It does not consider [[Crop Fortune]], but the crop drops are 1.5x of those from a regular Pest.
Killing a Pest also rewards the player with 5 [[Bits]], multiplied by their [[Fame Ranks|Bits Multiplier]].
### Rare Drops
All non-guaranteed Pest drops have their drop rate increased by [[Overbloom]].
The formula for drop chance for rare drops is:
Drop Chance=Base Drop Chance×(1+Overbloom100)
For pet drops ( [[Rat Pet]] and [[Slug Pet]]), the formula also includes [[Pet Luck]]:
Drop Chance=Base Drop Chance×(1+Overbloom100+Pet Luck600)
All Pests can drop their respective Feast RARE CROP if it is in-season. Field Mice have a 30% chance to drop one of the in-season crop's RARE CROP at random.

### Hunting
All Pests can be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or [[Lassos|Lassoed]] to obtain their [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]]. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Tips
- The maximum damage that can be dealt to Pests requires an [[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] reforged with [[Clipped Wings]] and 5 [[Bookworm's Favorite Book]] applied, [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] equipped, [[Attributes|Insect Power X]], and the [[Book of Progression]] on Extra Infliction.
  - This deals 2,200 [[Damage]][Confirm] damage before factoring in the [[Book of Progression]].
## Trivia
- While most non-Pest-specific damage sources do not grant extra damage towards Pests, the [[Book of Progression]]'s Extra Infliction ability does.
- The base mob type is a [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bat]] for Pests that can fly ( [[Fly]], [[Mosquito]], [[Moth]]) and [[Silverfish (disambiguation)|Silverfish]] for the rest.
- If a flying or a running Pest manages to get out of the plot it spawned in, it will be teleported back into the correct plot within a few seconds. Jumping Pests return inside the plot on their own unless they cannot, in which case they too get teleported.
- During testing on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], [[Pesthunter Phillip]] allowed an option to prevent a Pest from spawning during [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]].
## Bugs
- Even though lassoing a mob does not do damage, when hunting Pests a damage indicator appears. The damage indicator is affected by [[Crit Damage]] and [[Strength]].
## Notes
- The bonus from [[The Garden|Garden]] bestiary functions differently with different effects:
  - Each tier of each of the mobs, including [[Timestalk Clone]] and [[Zombuddy]] grants 0.4 , up to a total of 100 [[Farming Fortune]].
  - [[Hedgehog Pet]]'s Hunter's Insight and [[Brown Bandana]] ignore the above mobs (as they're not Pests), leading to a total of 157.5 [[Farming Fortune]] and 45 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] respectively.

# Phantom Spirit
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Events, Spooky Festival
[Phantom Spirit]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 92
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 10; [[Damage]]: 50
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Green Candy]] 0–1x [[Purple Candy]] 0–1x [[Ectoplasm]] 0–1x [[Midnight Dye]]
XP: +138 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 101
[Metadata]

The Phantom Spirit is a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]], found only in places such as the [[Graveyard]], [[Wilderness]], [[Spider's Den]], and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]. This mob runs at the player and attacks rapidly. It copies the player's [[Armor]], Skin, and [[Weapons|Weapon]].

## Trivia
- There was a glitch where, if the player were to use a [[Swords|Sword]] with the [[Thunderlord]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]], the Lightning strike would instantly kill the Phantom Spirit similar to dealing [[True Damage]], ignoring all its [[Defense]].
- When it transforms, it does not copy the player's Weapon and Armor [[Stats]].
  - However, the Thorns enchantment will still function on the Phantom Spirit, if the player has it on their armor.
- Both [[Timestalk Clone]] and Phantom Spirit take the skin of the player. The former upon spawning, while the latter when attacking a player.

The Pitfighter is a type of [[Mob]] and [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblin]] that spawns in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]], belonging to the [[Goblin Raid|Goblin Raiders]] bestiary.
## Spawning
Pitfighters spawn during the [[Goblin Raid|Goblin Raids]] events in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and in the [[Goblin Holdout]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] in certain structures.
## Behavior
- Pitfighters attack players with melee hits.
- They do not run away from players, unlike some other [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblins]].
- Their walking speed is remarkably slow.

# Powder Ghast
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Dwarven Mines
[Powder Ghast]

ID: POWDER_GHAST_1
The Powder Ghast is a mob that spawns randomly in [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Spawning
The Powder Ghast has a 0.02% (1 in 5,000) chance to spawn when any player on [[Dwarven Mines]] breaks a [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]]. Powder Ghast spawning has a 10m cooldown. Spawn chance can be boosted with Luck of the Cave and Tunnel Vision [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM]] abilities.
Powder Ghast can spawn in one of the following locations: [[Divan's Gateway]], [[Rampart's Quarry]], [[Cliffside Veins]] or [[Forge Basin]].
## Behavior
The Powder Ghast flies around the spawn location, while trying to fly away from players. If the Powder Ghast is not killed whitin a minute, it will de-spawn. De-spawn timer resets after each hit. The Powder Ghast cannot take melee hits.
## Loot

|[[Coins]]|18|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|82|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|60|
The Powder Ghast also drops 100-250 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] with each hit, which totals to 17,500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] total on average.

## Bugs
- A Powder Ghast can be [[Cocooning|cocooned]], which makes it immune to ranged damage, in addition to its normal melee damage immunity.
  - This makes the cocooned Powder Ghast effectively unkillable and it will simply despawn after a minute.
## Tips
- Bows are the only efficient way to attack it since it is immune to magic and melee damage.
- By using [[Spirit Boots]] or [[Tarantula Boots]], players are able to stay close to it and damage it.
- [[Ferocity]] impacts the hits done, so items like [[Werewolf Armor]] and [[Tiger Pet]] can help do more damage.

# Power Dragon
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Power Dragon]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 765
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]; [[Ender]]; [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000; [[Defense]]: 2,500; [[Damage]]: 720,000
[Drops]
XP: +1,147 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 841
[Metadata]
ID: MASTER_WITHER_KING_DRAGON_100
The Power Dragon, also named Withered Dragon, is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the final phase of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss fight.
## Boss Fight
The bottom of Master Mode Floor VII contains a giant throne room. There are five Relics placed next to Dragon statues around the room. There are also five cauldrons in front of the throne near the center of the room. They each have a specifically colored Clay below them. The players must bring and place the Relics with the same corresponding color as the Cauldron's block of clay. By doing so, The Wither King will be summoned on his throne.
Damage cannot be dealt towards The Wither King, and he mostly acts as a spectator to the battle. The Wither King spawns five dragons with special abilities, including the Power Dragon. All five dragons must be killed near their corresponding colored Dragon statue to destroy them. All Dragons can respawn at any time indefinitely until all dragon statues are destroyed. If all five statues are destroyed, the floor is completed.
## Behavior
The Power Dragon will fly to the center of the throne room to charge up a TNT attack. Unless enough Damage is dealt to the Power Dragon before it starts its attack, it will begin to drop ignited [[TNT]], dealing 4x the base [[Damage]] (2,880,000 ).

## Spawning

Primordial Bats slowly float around, dealing 0.1% (Tier IV) / 0.2% (Tier V) of the player's max [[Health]] as [[Damage]] every second. They are also immune to [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]].

The Primordial Hatchling is a [[Mob]] that can spawn when fighting a Tier III or Tier IV [[Tarantula Broodfather]].
## Spawning
During the Tier III and Tier IV [[Tarantula Broodfather]] fight, it will use its Web of Lies ability at 66% and 33% [[Health]]. This will cause it to spawn 2 (Tier III) / 3 (Tier IV) eggs sacs above it, with 3 (Tier III) / 6 (Tier IV). If the eggs aren't shot down in time, they will hatch into a Primordial Hatchling.
## Behavior
Primordial Hatchlings run towards the player to attack them.

# Puff
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Confirmations needed
[Puff]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 7
Spawn Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 100
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Puff Crux]]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: PUFF_7
A Puff is a [[Mob]] that spawns on [[The Mountaintop]]. It appears as a floating head.
## Spawning
Puffs are passive and have a chance to be spawned by player movement while on [[The Mountaintop]]. The spawn chance increases the higher the player goes[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]. The [[Gravity Potion|Gravity I Splash Potion]] bought from the [[The Mountaintop|Vending Machine]] increases the spawn rate of Puffs around the player.
Puffs spawn one block above a moving player's head then proceed to rise another 5 blocks over the course of a few seconds. It then hovers at that height (~8 blocks above ground level) for the remainder of its lifetime. Puffs can hover higher than 8 blocks depending on the player's position when it spawned, such as when jumping. From the moment a Puff spawns, it will remain for ~100 seconds before despawning, making a soft 'pop' sound when it does.
## Behavior
Puffs do not attack the player. Nearby Puffs will connect with others indicated by blue particles between them. Killing a Puff will cause connected Puffs to die in a chain reaction, increasing the drop rate of [[Puff Crux|Puff Cruxes]]. Puffs can also be damaged with a [[Berberis Blowgun]].
## Drops
The drop rate of [[Puff Crux]] is increased by 10% every time a linked Puff dies, up to 100%. [[Puff Crux]] drops are also boosted by the [[Crux Talisman]] and its upgrades.
Item drops - [Lv7]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Puff Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv7]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|50|50|50|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
### Loot Sharing
[Confirm]
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
Item drops - [Lv7]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Puff Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%|
## Trivia
- Popping a link of 10 Puffs will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"Zoop!" Achievement]].
- Unlike other crux mobs, a Puff remains passive when damaged by a player.
